{MORDOR_RING_AI_BODY}Lejos llegan las garras de Mordor en estos días aciagos. Nos han llegado noticias de que algunos de sus aliados de confianza le han entregado el Anillo Único y pronto se alzará y dominará la Tierra Media.
{MORDOR_RING_AI_TITLE}¡Los sirvientes de Sauron le entregan el anillo!
{SARUMAN_MANYCOLOURS_DIES_BODY}The sun rises to join its place in a sapphire sky as the realms of the Free Peoples rejoice. Just this morn, news came from the Orthanc. Even the Elves, within their trees and hidden glades, cannot help but rejoice, sing a song of praise to Elbereth as the news spreads across the land of Middle-earth: Saruman of Many Colours is dead!\n\nThe Maia had come as a guide, sent by the Valar away in the heavenly lands of Valinor, choosing to forsake the easy existence in Aman, to be stripped of his power, and to be sent to the war-torn regions of Middle-earth alongside four others. Their goal was simple: to guide the Free Peoples to the final and utter defeat of the Dark Lord. Saruman the White was the greatest among these, Curunír, the Man of Skill, he was known, and his voice was soothing like a lover’s embrace, sweet as honey. Yet the good will would not last. While the guides sent from Valinor forming the Order of the Istari were stripped of their power in order to not be tempted to fall awry in their task, Saruman the White kept a secret envy in his black heart. This envy of one of his own order grew, until he would prove that he was the greatest of the Istari, that he could be Lord. After all, why should he merely guide when he was born to lead?\n\nSaruman delved into the arts of Ring-crafting, becoming obsessed with the One Ring forged by the Dark Lord in the fires of Orodruin. This preoccupation led Saruman down a dark road, culminating in his theft of the creation of a hideous breed of Sauron’s greatest warriors, the Black Uruks. With his own servants, Saruman declared himself a rival to the Great Eye, coming like a storm upon Rohan, easily sweeping away the resistance of the peaceful folk of the plains. Yet at the last, Saruman’s obsessions had paid off: he had discovered the One Ring to Rule Them All. At last, the greatest of all artefacts, the Ring above all Rings of Power, was his. With it, he bent even the Black Riders to his will, stealing them from Mordor’s service and into the command of a new Dark Lord; the Iron Fortress ruled over the lands Saruman conquered, cruelly and harshly sapping will and joy wherever he went.\n\nBut pride goeth before the fall, and Saruman is no different. Some say the Valar themselves were the ones who ordained it to be, that the yolk Saruman cast upon those he conquered will be thrust upon him, ten times heavier. But in the aftermath of the death of Saruman, one thing is clear: all beings, whether they Free or servants of the true Dark Lord, may breathe easier in the knowledge of the death of a tyrant, and the end of a Maia.
{SARUMAN_MANYCOLOURS_DIES_TITLE}¡Saruman de Muchos Colores yace derrotado!
{SAURON_DIES_BODY}¡Sauron, el Engañador, ha sido derrotado! Un antiguo ser conocido por muchos nombres, Sauron ha sido el principal adversario de los Pueblos Libres durante casi toda su existencia. La historia se repite, ¡pues el Señor Oscuro en su forma física ha sido abatido! Ya se había logrado antes, pero sólo gracias a los esfuerzos combinados de los legendarios reyes Gil-galad y Elendil, que perecieron en el acto. Los rumores de la gran hazaña de un poderoso guerrero llegan a todos los rincones de la Tierra Media, pero cómo llegó a suceder probablemente se pierda en el tiempo. Con su forma física destruida, Sauron debe retirarse y reconstruir su fuerza en su fortaleza de Barad-dûr, tomando la forma de un Gran Ojo que todo lo ve, sin párpado y envuelto en llamas, terrible de contemplar. Las vastas huestes de los Orcos se estremecen de miedo, pues la indomable voluntad que los impulsa flaquea, y los Pueblos Libres sienten renovada su esperanza cuando la sombra se aleja de la tierra. Sin embargo, para derrotar a semejante poder se necesita un acto final: arrancar el Anillo Único de la mano negra. Todos los pensamientos de los Sabios se inclinan hacia esto, ¿ha sido reclamado el Anillo de Poder o se ha perdido una vez más? Llevar semejante carga y enfrentarse a la corrupción del Anillo sólo puede conducir a la ruina y a la muerte. Llevar este Anillo durante demasiado tiempo es olvidar el sabor del pan, el sonido de los árboles, la suavidad del viento. Incluso tu propio nombre...
{SAURON_DIES_TITLE}¡Sauron ha sido vencido!
{RING_DESTROYED_FAILED_BODY}The fate of Middle-earth now hangs in the balance as our mission to destroy the One Ring has come to a crushing end. Despite our valiant efforts, our hero who bravely sought to infiltrate the enemy's territory and cast the ring into the fiery depths of Mount Doom has been captured by Sauron's forces. With the ring now in the hands of the dark lord, his dominion over Middle-earth is all but certain. The armies of Mordor will soon march forth to crush all those who stand in their way, leaving the free peoples of Middle-earth to face a bleak and uncertain future. It is up to us now to regroup, plan anew, and find a way to defeat the overwhelming power of the enemy and bring hope back to our world.
{RING_DESTROYED_FAILED_TITLE}¡Portador del anillo capturado!
{RING_DESTROYED_FAILED_EVIL_BODY}The news of the infiltrator had quickly spread throughout the lands of Mordor. The orcs and other minions of the Dark Lord had been on high alert, constantly on the lookout for any signs of intrusion. When the spy was finally captured, his fate was sealed. He was brought before Sauron himself, who ordered him to be tortured for information. But to no avail, as the spy remained loyal to his cause and refused to reveal any details. It was only when the ring was discovered on his person that Sauron's interest was piqued. The ring was like no other the orcs had seen before, with a golden shimmer that hinted at its incredible power. The Dark Lord recognized it immediately as the One Ring, forged in the fires of Mount Doom. He ordered that the ring be brought to him at once, determined to claim it for himself and ensure that no one else could ever wield its power against him. Sauron's quest for domination is one step closer to completion.

{PLAYER_KEEPS_RING_EVIL_BODY}The decision to keep the One Ring was not taken lightly. It was a momentous occasion that split the faction in two, with many arguing vehemently for the ring's return to Sauron while others saw its immense power as too valuable to ignore. But with the decision made, the faction now faces a grim future. Sauron, the Dark Lord, will not take kindly to the theft of his most precious possession, and his wrath will soon be unleashed upon the faction. It is a dark and dangerous time, and the faction must be prepared to defend itself against the full force of Sauron's armies. The fate of Middle-earth hangs in the balance, and the stakes have never been higher.
{PLAYER_KEEPS_RING_EVIL_TITLE}You keep the One Ring!
{PLAYER_KEEPS_RING_GOOD_BODY}The decision to keep the Ring was not taken lightly. There were heated debates, arguments, and even some threats were made. However, in the end, our council decided that the Ring was too powerful to give away to the Elves and their allies. Why should they dictate what the rightful owners of the Ring should do with it? It was a gift, after all, and they believed they had the right to use it to their advantage.\n\nSome worried about the consequences of such a decision. What if Sauron found out? Would he not be even more enraged, and would his fury not be directed at them? But our council remained resolute. They are confident that they could control the Ring's power and wield it to their own ends. With the decision made, preparations began for the Ring's use. Plans were laid out, alliances were made, and troops were mustered. Our leaders are determined to show the world that they are not to be underestimated. They would become the most powerful faction in all of Middle-earth, and the Ring would be the key to their success. Little did they know that their decision would have dire consequences. The Ring was not a gift, but a curse. And the power it bestowed upon its wielder came at a great cost. Sauron was not the only one who would tremble at the sight of their strength. For the Ring would consume them, corrupt them, and eventually destroy them.

You have no intention of handing over the Ring, especially not to some outsider who thinks they know better than you. With the Ring in your possession, you will become the strongest power in all of Middle-earth, and no one will be able to stop you. You will harness the Ring's power to bend all to your will and establish yourself as the true ruler of the land. His armies, already formidable, will become invincible with the power of the ring behind them. The Dark Lord's triumph seems assured, and none can foresee the end of his reign. {COUNCIL_START_BODY}My Lord, we have arrived in Imladris, also known as Rivendell or the last Homely House. This is where Elrond and the White Council resides. Its inhabitants have rushed us inside, not giving us the time to take in the mesmerising sights of this peaceful valley. In such a hurry they are, over the One Ring, that for once Elven hospitality falls short and we are barely afford any time to rest our laurels from the long journey.\n\nWithin hours of our arrival, the White Council convenes. Ancient Elves, wise Wizards, mighty Dwarves and honoured Men sit together and argue back and forth on what to do with Sauron's treacherous craft. Many different opinions are heard. Some wish to hide it, far away from the Dark Lord's prying eyes. Others wish to wield it and use its power against its master. In the end a decision is made and Elrond, leader of the Council, approaches us. Whether through show of might or subterfuge, we are to take the One Ring to the south and to the east, deep into the dark lands of Mordor, into Mount Doom. There the one Ring was made. Only there can it be unmade. We must take it there and cast it deep into the fiery chasm from whence it came!

The One Ring bestows immense power upon those with a will strong enough to wield but it also carries ruin and great danger with it. If we wish to destroy it, we must take it before the White Council in Imladris or Mithlond if Imladris is no longer safe. The White Council will know what is the best course of action and if we are to destroy it, we will need all the help we can get. If we decide to keep it however, we will most likely have to endure the wrath of the other Free Peoples for keeping such a powerful tool like this for ourselves. The risk of it falling into the hands of the Dark Lord is likely a risk they are not willing to take..
{ONE_RING_ACQUIRED_GOOD_TITLE}¡El Anillo Único ha sido hallado!
{ONE_RING_ACQUIRED_ISENGARD_BODY}¡Mi Señor! ¡Hemos conseguido el Anillo Único! Tras largos años de búsqueda por la Tierra Media, el anillo está ahora en nuestras manos. El anillo resuena con su poderosa influencia... una influencia oscura, que puede cambiar el destino de muchos.\nEl Anillo Único debe ser llevado a Isengard para que su poder tenga pleno efecto en el Dominio. Una vez que el anillo esté asegurado, el Mago Blanco podrá doblegar la voluntad de los Nazgûl a las órdenes de Sauron, blandiéndolos como una herramienta de la oscuridad, para limpiar la tierra. Si no entregamos el anillo, el Mago Blanco, el Señor Oscuro y las fuerzas del bien se volverán contra nosotros.
{ONE_RING_ACQUIRED_ISENGARD_TITLE}¡El Anillo Único ha sido hallado!
{ONE_RING_ACQUIRED_SARUMAN_BODY}¡Mi Señor! ¡Hemos conseguido el Anillo Único! Tras largos años de búsqueda por la Tierra Media, el anillo está ahora en nuestras manos. El anillo resuena con su poderosa influencia... una influencia oscura, que puede cambiar el destino de muchos.\nPodemos quedarnos con el Anillo Único y ejercer su todopoderoso poder para nosotros. Una vez conseguido el anillo, podremos doblegar la voluntad de los Nazgûl a las órdenes de Sauron, manejándolos como una herramienta de la oscuridad, para limpiar la tierra. Otros efectos del anillo serán estudiados una vez que llegue a Isengard. Sin embargo, esto nos volverá contra el todopoderoso Sauron, el legítimo dueño del anillo, y sus aliados.
{ONE_RING_ACQUIRED_SARUMAN_TITLE}¡El Anillo Único ha sido hallado!
{SARUMAN_RING_DEMAND_BODY}A messenger from Isengard has come to us with an urgent request from the White Wizard. We are told to be on the lookout for a plain golden ring that turns its wearer invisible. The White Wizard claims it is a valuable Elvish weapon that would greatly aid us in our war efforts. However, he is vague about its purpose.\n\nDespite the lack of details, the prospect of a powerful weapon to aid our cause is too enticing to ignore. Many of our warriors have set out on a quest to find this magical ring, believing it to be a simple and unassuming object.\n\nThose who do find the ring are instructed to bring it immediately to the tower of Orthanc. But beware, we do not know what dangers may await us on this quest. Trust no one and be on guard for any who may try to steal the ring from us. The fate of our people may depend on our success.
{SARUMAN_RING_DEMAND_TITLE}Un mensaje de Orthanc
{GANDALF_RING_WARNING_BODY}Yesterday an old wizard wearing grey worn out robes arrived for an audience with our leader. His message was to look out for a creature called "Gollum", supposedly looking like a starved small Orc and frequently making a sound that sounds like what he has been called: "Gollum". If we cannot find this twisted creature, we should look for a golden ring that he supposedly possesses. According to the wizard, it will be useful for our fight against the power of Sauron. He says we must look to our neighbours and find out if they could have possession of this ring, as it falling into the wrong hands could have catastrophic consequences for the future of Middle-earth.
{GANDALF_RING_WARNING_TITLE}Un mensaje de Gandalf
{SAURON_RING_DEMAND_BODY}The messenger from Mordor came with great urgency, his dark eyes fixed on our leader as he spoke. He bore the mark of Sauron on his armour, and his words carried the weight of the Dark Lord's will. He told us of a simple ring, unassuming in appearance but powerful beyond measure. A ring that could turn its wearer invisible to all but the most powerful of eyes.\n\nThe messenger did not explain why Sauron desired the ring, but his tone brooked no argument. We knew that disobedience to the Dark Lord meant certain death, so we pledged our loyalty and obedience to the task. We would scour the lands, search every nook and cranny, and find this ring. And once we had it, we would bring it to the Dark Tower of Lugbúrz without delay, where Sauron himself would claim it.\n\nAs we prepared for the hunt, whispers began to spread among us. What could such a ring do in the hands of the Dark Lord? What power would it grant him? And if we failed to find it, what punishment would befall us? But such thoughts were quickly quelled, for to doubt the will of Sauron was to invite his wrath. And so we set out, with fear in our hearts but the promise of reward in our minds.
{SAURON_RING_DEMAND_TITLE}Un mensaje de Mordor
{RING_ERUPTION_BODY}Barad-dûr, la Torre Oscura, ha caído y las tierras de Mordor han sido purgadas de la influencia de Sauron y sus siervos. El Señor Oscuro ha perdido su base más fuerte y su refugio en la Tierra Media.

¬##### Script: Last Stand
{LAST_STAND_DUNLAND_BODY}Los hombres de Dunland han conocido incontables penurias. Obligados a abandonar sus hogares por los Rohirrim, su propia existencia como pueblo se encuentra ahora en el filo de la navaja. Un pueblo belicoso y resistente, los Dunlendinos se han negado a rendirse en silencio a su desaparición, y han formado una poderosa hueste de muchos guerreros y hombres de clan. Pronto, sus enemigos sabrán que el hacha de las Tierras Brunas aún tiene su filo: ¡a la batalla, a la gloria, a la muerte!
{LAST_STAND_DUNLAND_TITLE}El mordisco del hacha
{LAST_STAND_DORWINION_BODY}A medida que los cuernos de la guerra se acercan a los fértiles viñedos, transportando las cenizas de cientos de batallas por toda la Tierra Media, el suelo mismo de Dorwinion se estremece. Hombres y los casi olvidados elfos avari, procedentes de todos los rincones de esta joven y fértil tierra, dejan atrás sus Viñedos y Bosques para tomar las armas y defender su amada Tierra entre el río y el mar. Innumerables bodegas de vino, ahora convertidas en armerías, son un recuerdo lejano. La pasión que el pueblo de Dorwinion llevó a lo largo de los siglos se convierte ahora en una ira implacable. Susurros de una esperanza moribunda silban entre plebeyos y nobles mientras marchan como uno solo; sus colores rojos como el vino.
{LAST_STAND_DORWINION_TITLE}La última copa de vino
{LAST_STAND_DA_BODY}Los hombres y mujeres de Dol Amroth han tomado las armas en un último intento por salvar su amada ciudad. Alfareros, panaderos, herreros, tejedores, todos los que de otro modo serían campesinos a los ojos de muchos hoy se alzan como guerreros. Los señores y nobles de las cortes de Dol Amroth lucharán junto a los labradores y campesinos para garantizar la supervivencia de todo lo que aprecian. Quienquiera que intente aplastar al navío y al cisne tendrá una última fuerza de armas a la que someter antes de que pueda asegurarse la aniquilación total de los amrothianos.
{LAST_STAND_DA_TITLE}La última batalla de Dol Amroth
{LAST_STAND_ISENGARD_BODY}Los enemigos de Isengard se han envalentonado y el Mago Blanco prepara su defensa final. Los sonidos de armaduras y armas se oyen por todas las tierras mientras los Uruks comienzan sus rituales. Saruman reúne a todas las criaturas disponibles que tiene a su alcance. Hombres malvados, uruk-hai, orcos, medio-orcos y huargos; todos serán convocados en la última defensa de Orthanc, y a partir de esto, el Mago Blanco intentará desesperadamente recuperar lo que era suyo. La horda afila sus cuchillas, sus picas y sus escudos, y los innumerables sonidos de pies marchando y de armas repiqueteando se oyen a kilómetros de distancia. Que aquellos que lo oigan, lo teman, pues los Uruks obtendrán la victoria, ¡o morirán en el intento!
{LAST_STAND_ISENGARD_TITLE}La batalla final de Orthanc
{LAST_STAND_MISTY_BODY}Destrozados y maltrechos, los orcos de las Montañas Nubladas han sufrido derrota tras derrota por todos lados. Acosados por sus enemigos, sus fuerzas son una mera fracción de lo que una vez fueron. Con cada vez menos posesiones montañosas en las que refugiarse, los orcos de las Montañas Nubladas convocan a todos, desde el más humilde de los snaga hasta el más poderoso trol de las cavernas. Estas fuerzas responden a la llamada a las armas y tratan de detener a todos los que purgan a los trasgos de sus posesiones montañosas, o mueren en el intento.
{LAST_STAND_MISTY_TITLE}¡Las cavernas son vaciadas!
{LAST_STAND_EREDLUIN_BODY}Hace siglos, las Montañas Azules fueron destrozadas y las grandes ciudades de Nogrod y Belegost destruidas por completo. Tras muchas generaciones, los enanos de las Ered Luin han recuperado por fin su gloria ancestral mientras sus ciudades cavan hondo y construyen grandes obras. Sin embargo las obras de los enanos están amenazadas y muchos enanos yacen muertos en las heladas llanuras del norte. Ahora toda la fuerza que le queda al reino reforjado debe ser reunida, todas las fuerzas ceremoniales y de reserva comprometidas y todos los artesanos lo suficientemente fuertes como para empuñar un arma reunidos. La voluntad y la gloria de los enanos pueden ser destrozadas por los enemigos que marchan hacia ellos, pero se enfrentarán a ellos tan resueltamente como sus antepasados se enfrentaron al gran dragón Glaurung y a las fuerzas de Morgoth de antaño.
{LAST_STAND_EREDLUIN_TITLE}La resolución de las Ered Luin
{LAST_STAND_LORIEN_BODY}The strength of the Galadhrim wanes and many have fallen in battle. Through the power of her mirror the lady has seen the fall of her people and has called all to take up their bows and swords in a final defence of the forest. Those who have not seen combat in millennia shall march forth and turn the last vestiges of their terrible power against the enemy.
{LAST_STAND_LORIEN_TITLE}La apuesta de Galadriel
{LAST_STAND_KHAND_BODY}Durante mucho tiempo, los Variags de Khand han vivido lejos de cualquiera de las grandes batallas de la tercera edad. Sus dominios eran intocables para sus enemigos. Sin embargo, un enemigo de los Variags se ha enfrentado directamente a ellos y ha amenazado con borrarlos del este. Ahora, los jefes de Khand se han reunido en su momento más calamitoso para hacer retroceder esta invasión de sus tierras y mostrar a estos invasores la ira de los Variags.
{LAST_STAND_KHAND_TITLE}Furia de las estepas
{LAST_STAND_ROHAN_BODY}Los días se extinguen en el oeste. Con la llegada de tiempos desesperados, los hombres de la Marca se han alzado en armas: hachas y lanzas en las manos de todos los hombres, desde los que apenas tienen unos pocos pelos en la barbilla hasta los barbiluengos más canosos. Junto a ellos están los eorlingas de Rohan, vestidos con armaduras de malla, montados en buenos caballos, dispuestos a acabar con quienes quieran arrebatarles las llanuras de Calenardhon. Esta última cabalgada desesperada verá la muerte o la gloria para los hombres de Eorl, y la puesta de un rojo amanecer sobre su pueblo.
{LAST_STAND_ROHAN_TITLE}La última carga de los Rohirrim
{LAST_STAND_WOODLAND_BODY}El reino de Thranduil siente cada vez más la presión de la oscuridad. La sombra ha despertado y criaturas oscuras han reclamado para sí la tierra de Eryn Galen. Pero el pueblo silvano no abandonará su hogar a la ligera. Reunidos detrás de sus señores Sindar han reunido un ejército del tamaño que no se había visto desde que Oropher marchó a Dagorlad. Que tiemblen las huestes enemigas, pues el Bosque Negro pronto se oscurecerá, mientras los arqueros del Reino del Bosque borran el sol con sus flechas.
{LAST_STAND_WOODLAND_TITLE}Una segunda Dagorlad
{LAST_STAND_EREBOR_BODY}Pocos pueblos han sufrido como el pueblo de Durin, y una vez más este resistente clan se ha visto acosado por enemigos de gran fuerza. Sin embargo, no es propio de los barbiluengos caer en la desesperación: el pueblo llano de Erebor ha cogido el hacha y el escudo, el arco y el yelmo, para ponerse al lado de sus parientes guerreros y rechazar la invasión. Un hogar una vez perdido está doblemente defendido, y la terquedad de los enanos bien podría ver derrotados los planes del enemigo. Sin embargo, esta es su última esperanza, y si caen ahora, nunca más tendrá este robusto pueblo un verdadero hogar.
{LAST_STAND_EREBOR_TITLE}La tozudez de los enanos
{LAST_STAND_BREE_BODY}Con la pacífica existencia de los hombres de Bree y los hobbits de la Comarca al borde de la ruina y la destrucción, corresponde a todos los hombres y hobbits por igual tomar las armas en defensa de sus hogares y su forma de vida. Cazadores y leñadores de los bosques, granjeros y sheriffs de la Comarca y los mercaderes y habitantes de la ciudad de Bree responden a la llamada. Puede que estos hombres de paz y prosperidad no hayan formado parte de las naciones acostumbradas a la guerra, pero con su moneda han reunido algunas armas a lo largo de los años que se entregan sin problemas a cualquiera lo bastante valiente como para usarlas. Los herreros abren sus almacenes de hachas y garfios, cuchillos y sartenes. Carentes de formación, pero armados con las herramientas de su oficio, estas gentes lucharán por sus hogares y su propia existencia con la valentía de quienes no tienen otro lugar al que huir.
{LAST_STAND_BREE_TITLE}La última batalla de Bree
{LAST_STAND_DOLGULDUR_BODY}La fortaleza de Sauron en el Bosque Negro está amenazada por todos lados, su último general ha solicitado una fuerza de la Torre Oscura de Barad-dûr, de su amo, y obtendrá un poderoso ejército. Reforzado por sus propias criaturas viciosas, orcos, espectros y trolls, pretende recuperar la tierra que una vez dominó. Porque las puntas envenenadas de los arqueros sentirán la sangre élfica, las malvadas y toscas espadas desgarrarán la carne del hombre, y los trols serán liberados para aniquilar a cualquier enemigo que se cruce en su camino. La hueste de Dol Guldur marchará dejando un sangriento rastro de desolación a su paso.
{LAST_STAND_DOLGULDUR_TITLE}La batalla final de Dol Guldur
{LAST_STAND_ANGMAR_BODY}Derrotadas una y otra vez, las fuerzas de Angmar no ven esperanza. Pero hay un orco que resistiría hasta el final. Esto le recuerda a la horda lo que podría ser: una tierra libre del látigo, libre del trabajo, libre del daño que muchos arrojarían sobre ellos. Después de la matanza de hace 1000 años, no hay orco en el norte que no tema que se repita. Sin embargo, ver a este desafiante orco enciende las brasas en el oscuro corazón de la sombra, pues esta horda no descansará hasta que la gloria de Angmar sea restaurada, ¡y todos los que nieguen a los angmarinos lo que quieren morirán!
{LAST_STAND_ANGMAR_TITLE}¡La ira angmarina renovada!
{LAST_STAND_UMBAR_BODY}Mientras los enemigos de los númenoreanos negros los empujan de vuelta a su puerto, los hombres malvados recuerdan... recuerdan la imagen de Thorongil, recuerdan el dolor que causó, el daño que infligió. Con un rugiente desafío, hasta el último soldado de Umbar se une, no para conquistar, sino para vengarse. Se unen para rechazar a los sucesores de Thorongil: "¡No volveréis a tenernos!", gritan y vitorean. Con una fuerza nunca vista en ellos, los umbarinos saben que prefieren morir a volver a ver la derrota.
{LAST_STAND_UMBAR_TITLE}¡Venganza de los númenoreanos incumplida!

He wishes to be our friend, so his emissaries claim, and sees the great injustices done to us by the Forgoil as debt in dire need of repayment.
The men that bring his word are of the Isendraich, a tribe of mix-bloods that dwell around and even inside his walls. They are keen-minded and knowledgeable, and make for good advisors to any Brehin, and the Gurach too. In exchange for sword and shield and wisdom, Saruman asks only that we send him our warriors to fight in Rohan, and for him to be allowed to build places where his followers can dwell, and our warriors can come to be equipped.\n\nThe Brehins rejoice at this. They say the Wise One uses his silver tongue to make us think he wishes only for our strength, when truly he hungers for our spirit. They say they won’t offer their guidance to Brehins that allow the White Hands banner to fly next to their Crebain.\n\nA difficult situation. The choice is yours, Gurach.

He has gathered the best of their number, the most hardened and loyal veterans, and formed them into a personal retinue, that heeds only his word. They wear armour of the finest make; iron lamellar, scale, and even Rohirric chain mail. The Skreulingir they are called, those born of war, and these are men whose only harvest are the heads of our foes.\n\nTruly, we are now a Realm that must not shy away from comparison with those of other races of men, and the future is bright.

Desde el sur sonaban tambores de guerra, y su sonido se hacía más doloroso a medida que crecía la sombra. A través de todo, la luz del oeste comenzó a menguar.\nSin embargo, antes de que la oscuridad pudiera consumirlo todo, apareció una nueva luz. La misma que había visto por primera vez en el oeste. Esta vez brillaba desde el norte, y con ella llegó la voz de una hermosa doncella, que cantaba y conjuraba nuevas imágenes junto con los tonos de luz.\n\nBusca la justa espada quebrada,\nen la Casa del Gran Elrond reside;\nallí un concilio se reune\npara desbaratar los hechizos de Morgul.\nMareas de perdición traerá.\nque el Destino está cerca:\nla Tercera Era pronto terminará,\ny el destino el Mediano decidirá.\n\nLe ruega que le permita viajar a Imladris y buscar el significado de su sueño. ¿Le permitirá emprender el peligroso viaje, mi Señor?"

Reports have surfaced of strange men, bearing a strong resemblance to the orcs, coming and going from the area.\n\nThe people of Middle-earth tremble with fear, uncertain of what the future may hold for the Hobbits and the surrounding regions. Only time will reveal what is truly happening in these once peaceful lands.

He promises a lot of gold and the protection of a professional military should we accept his offer. Although I must warn that I use the word offer lightly as it has been made quite clear that should we decline, his men will certainly resort to violence... Some have even speculated that Sharkey is none other than Saruman the White, who was last seen at Isengard before its fall. The influx of wild and orc-like men into the region has only fuelled these suspicions.\n\nDespite the growing danger, some still cling to the hope that Lotho can lead them out of these troubling times. They believe that he has a plan to protect the Shire from the coming war, and that they must trust him if they are to survive. Others are less certain, and fear that Lotho's actions will only bring further harm to their beloved homeland.

¬##### Script: Bree Choice
{BREE_CHOICE_WARNING_BODY}Mi señor, bajo su dirección el Consejo de Bree junto con el Thane y el Alcalde de Michel Delving iniciarán conversaciones sobre el destino de los Pueblos Libres de Eriador. Con la lentitud del gobierno y la famosa y obstinada presencia Hobbit creo que pasará un año entero antes de que nuestras conversaciones lleguen a algún tipo de decisión concluyente.
{BREE_CHOICE_WARNING_TITLE}El Consejo se reune
{BREE_INFLUENCE_CHOICE_BODY}M'lord, the nature of our talks became known to the Dúnedain and they sent a lordly messenger of their own, Pedhaer, to assist with the process. Now, after a full year of discussion, arguments, bitterness and no small amount of peacekeeping it is clear we have a choice of two paths to walk:\n\nThe first is perhaps the more feared yet most eloquently, yet pleadingly, requested by Pedhaer and that is to stand with the Dúnedain; shoulder to shoulder. He asked that we rise up and declare boldly and unabashedly that we, the Bree-landers and simple Hobbits of the Shire, will not stand for Sauron's dominion! He asked that we not see the blood of the Dúnedain, Elves and Dwarves be disrespected with our ignorance and wilful neglect of our own defence and that we fight for the freedom of Middle-earth. The council was at first horrified by his scornful words and the idea of marching to far corners of the world but some felt it was our duty to assist those who have defended us, unseen, for countless years. Pedhaer's suggestion was that we directly help fund the Dúnedain war effort; although this will mean a big monetary hit to our own coffers. However he said that much of that money would combine with our well developed infrastructure to ensure that our stout fighters would be equipped in the garb of Old Arnor! The White Tree would stand proud on the chests of every man who fights for our freedom. He also told of how the Dúnedain themselves would eagerly fight with us if we came out of, what he called, hiding and that together we would be nigh on unstoppable. Furthermore he spoke of a number of Elves of both Imladris and Lindon who would be so moved by our defiance that they too would stand with us against the shadow! Finally he recounted tales of the majesty of the Great Roads of Eriador and the mighty bridge at Tharbad and how we, with Dúnedain help, could rewrite those tales with great roads of our own! Pedhaer spoke of power, freedom, majesty and hope, but his talk of money didn't sit well with most and the Hobbits especially sneer at him even now for fear that their sons, brothers and fathers will be sent to war. Not an easy path to walk but surely it is the morally right one? Do we leave the Free Peoples to stand alone, should their blood be spilled whilst we grow fat and lazy? Are we truly ready to spill our own blood though? How will the Council face the wives and mothers of those sent outside Eriador, how will we face the wives and mothers of those sent that do not return?\n\nThe second path was greatly lauded by many as the 'perfect solution'; we simply pay others to do what we 'should not have to do'. These words were spoken by Dringol; a most stout, block-headed, stubborn, rude, cowardly, miserly, old and bothersome Hobbit. He was the most outspoken opponent of Pedhaer and he proposed a simple solution. He cried that we should look to our own borders, our builders should not be out restoring weed-infested old roads but instead should be building better fortifications here for our people. He felt that strong walls and not strong arms would be needed to stop the tide of darkness. He argued for the importance of maintaining our thriving economy and to do this on our own, not by letting every 'Tom, Dick or Harry who happens upon the Bree-land to waltz in!' His plans had the support of many of the councilmen for the promise of wealth was very real and where Pedhaer speaks of an overall decline in our wealth, Dringol proclaims that it can be made tenfold what it already is. Finally, the isolationists would have us buy our protection from cut-throats and bandits rather than risk our own sons on the field. They talk of how many travellers pass through the Bree-land asking for work and that we should put them to use defending our interests abroad. Under Dringol's plan mercenaries from the farthest reaches of Middle-earth would come to fight under our banner and our people will be safe. He even talks of Dwarven warriors from both Ered Luin and Khazad-dûm who have expressed an interest in fighting for us if the price is right! This path seems cowardly to me but the promise of safety, wealth, grand cities and others to do our dying seems almost too good to be true; doesn't it?\n\nAfter all this time a decision has to be made. The Council is split and only the word of our Master will set us on the path. So, do we side with Pedhaer and the Dúnedain or do we side with Dringol and the mercenaries? (Click on the tick to side with the Dúnedain and Elves or click on the cross to side with the Mercenaries and Dwarves)

Our nation will surely grow strong under this union and may all those who stand in defiance of the Shadow take note! We will not let the Shadow grow further, Eriador has risen anew. Ever onward we march, as powerful as Arnor of old, side by side with our brothers; the Dúnedain! Tûl Acharn! Tûl Acharn! Tûl Acharn!

Nuestra alianza invisible con ellos ha terminado y todos los que luchaban con nosotros han regresado a sus hogares en En Egladil. {BREE_CHOICE_DECLINED_TITLE}¡Estamos solos!

¬##### Script: Unite the Clans - Harad
{MUHAD_JOIN_BODY}The Mûhad are a nation of several tribes who occupy a large swath of territory within the more desolate regions of Near and Far Harad. They control the vital oasis of the southern deserts and maintain trade routes to the mysterious lands beyond. They are considered a savage people, but only because they choose to live as their ancestors did since the first age. They are respected by the Haradrim for their knowledge of the deserts and have always been a vital ally of the Serpent Tribe.\n\nThey join us once more! Being ever pragmatic, their leaders have watched us reassert our power and authority in the region and come to us offering a small host of warriors and beasts as a sign of respect and more will come, bolstering our armies with light skirmishes and auxiliary riders. A powerful chieftain joins us personally, eager for a chance at glory and to see that our expansion also benefits the Mûhad.
{MUHAD_JOIN_TITLE}¡Las tribus Mûhad se unen a la causa!
{TROLLMEN_JOIN_BODY}Excellent news, oh great one! The mighty Warchief Ubaàdah of the Troll-men has finally been convinced to join us and now his warband marches beneath our banner. It took a great deal of negotiation to finally bring this proud warrior into the fold, no offering of gold or power seemed to sway him but at last our envoy found something he desired — Death.\n\nIt would seem the Troll-men hold their brutal traditions in higher regard than any trinket or other, they worship their sacred Mumakil Gravyard not as a place of respect for the beasts themselves but as a monument of death itself. A gateway to the abyss. And so Ubaàdah and his people have been promised a new monument, one that they will create with the corpses of our enemies.\n\nWith massive blades of carved bone and heavy javelins these warriors act as devastating shock-troops. {TROLLMEN_JOIN_TITLE}¡Los hombres troll ofrecen su lealtad!
{HASHARII_JOIN_BODY}The Hasharii are a dark and secretive organization that traces its origin to the second age. During Numenor’s brutal occupation of Harad the Hasharii were the first warriors to organize against them, using stealth to overcome Numenorian strength. After the fall of Numenor the Hasharii became Sauron’s hand and enforcers of his divine will. And so they remain faithful to this day… for none have betrayed their greater purpose and lived to tell of it.\n\nThe true extent of the Hasharii’s power is unknown, but they have hidden holds and guilds scattered across Harad. Soon nothing will stand in our way…
{HASHARII_JOIN_TITLE}¡Los hasharii emergen de las sombras!
{HARAD_UNITED_BODY}Incredible news, my lord! Our ceaseless labor to bring the mighty Mûmakil into our ranks has finally been vindicated. Our agents have spent years establishing relations with the Troll-men, the only people known to hunt and train these creatures. At first they were resistant but over time they began to see our strength and the wisdom in helping our cause. Of course the Mûmakil has marched with our legions before, but this time is different, this time with the help of the Mûhad and countless laborers we have created a network secure pens and granaries to allow these noble animals to be transported from vast south into our heartlands. This will, over time, provide us with a steady means for bringing middle earths most powerful beasts to the frontlines of war. Our enemies will be crushed beneath their awesome power! No foe can deny us now.\n\nThe first of these beasts is ready for immediate deployment, lead by the architect of this effort as a gift for his loyalty and perseverance. Every life lost, every coin spent, was worth it. It is a monumental expense to build and maintain the logistical support these beasts require, but we have been unflinching in the face of this hardship. The people suffer what they must to secure our glorious future, for the horizon now glitters with untold prizes!\n\nTo add to this glorious day; teams of our scribes have made a great contribution to our future and have uncovered a hidden archive deep within Fuinor’s Catacombs. Scrolls and texts depict the original design of the Kingsblood Tributary, the massive irrigation system that was built by our Numenorian occupiers in the second age! The scribes believe that, with many thousands of workers, they can restore the Tributary and bring water from the Harnen into lands thought to desolate for crops. This will increase productivity of our farms ten fold! By your command, we shall begin this work immediately.\n\nWhile the Haradwaith celebrates the Serpent Lord plots even greater conquests and glory, we stand poised to take our final revenge upon west and establish a lasting order over all of middle earth.

¬##### Script: Harad Reform
{HARAD_KINGDOMS_BODY}Victory… such a beautiful thing when so complete, when delivered with such totality. Victory, sweet as wine or pleasurable company. It runs into the rivers and out to sea, to dilute into the fathomless depths that swallowed that most horrid of nations.\n\nVengeance… savor this now, let it warm you and lift yourself with such feeling.\n\nAnd as we rise we must marvel at what we have become to achieve such a feat. Our ancestors survived as tribes, huddled together along the Harnen, amongst the dunes and jungles. Never dreaming that their children could unite as we have, never once believing that they could become such a mighty realm of men! And yet, here we stand — upon the ashes of our hated foe, with the mantle of Kings at our feet.\n\nOn this day we shed our tribal past, and embrace our imperial destiny.\n\nHenceforth and for all eternity we shall claim our place on middle earth as the Empire of the Haradwaith!\n\nThe southern realms bow to their rightful rulers and the world trembles before our mighty legions. What glory awaits us in this new age? What conquests shall we make of the remaining nations? These questions are ours alone to answer…

¬##### Script: Blue Wizards

{BLUE_WIZARDS_WARNING_BODY}Khan, te traigo noticias que podrían tener consecuencias nefastas para nosotros si son ciertas. I fear we must make ourselves ready to receive them as we cannot fight them with such short notice.\n\nTaking your throne, you made ready to entertain the Blue Wizards. After a few minutes the two Istari swept into your tent, clad in robes of sky blue and clutching simple staffs carved of the dark wood of the plains, the two bowed to you and began to address you. "Oh Great Khan, leader of the diverse and mighty people of Khand, and your retinue; we are the Blue Wizards, I am Alatar and my companion is Pallando. We have come to you to ask of you a harrowing task, one that will be the most difficult that you have ever faced but in its undertaking your people shall become more prosperous and your memory shall be enshrined in your legends for ages to come. We ask for you to abandon Sauron and join us."\n\nAt the mention of the Dark Lord's name some of your retinue jeered at the heretical act the Istari had committed, others spat at the Istari due to their sheer absurdity of their request. Pallando continued, undeterred by the jeering, "We know this goes against all of your traditions and your beliefs, but you serve a lord who treats you as mere pieces upon the board, willing to throw away as many lives as it takes, without consideration... as long as he accomplishes his goals. Furthermore, he values you even less than he does his other servants - for you are divided and scattered and in his eyes you are nothing compared to the unified hammers of Harad and Rhûn; it is those two that shall be rewarded if Sauron claims dominion over Middle-earth. Your clan shall only be tossed scraps.\n\nIf you desert him, you can forge a new golden age for Khand, one in which you will be beholden to no one. Furthermore not only shall we and our host join you, for you are our final destination as Khan, but the Orocarni shall serve you and they shall grant you the secrets of the crossbow. If you do align with us however, we fear we dwell too close to the Shadow of Mordor and Sauron will easily be able to send an Inquisitor to crush our rebellion, hence, we shall need to fight against foes from every side. We know this is a fateful decision for you, so we shall give you the night to think over it." The two Istari and your retinue left your tent, leaving you with your advisor.\n\nKhan, I can see now why many clans joined them. Their offer is compelling and does ring true. However, I think if we were to deny them, the Dark Lord may reward us; perhaps with the knowledge of taming the Great Beasts. The Inquisitor the Istari spoke of would be granted to us as a reward, along with the service of his troops. I think he may be a Black Númenórean, so perhaps we would be granted the service of Mordor's famed Temple Legions. With such rewards from the Dark Tower, we could prove that we are worth more than scraps and defy the destiny they foretold of us under the Dark Lord, our people would even find you a truly great leader. I think with such prestige you may be able to command them to gather in even larger camps! In the end however, it is your decision, my Khan.\n\nIf we wish to accept the offer of the Istari click the green tick, if we refuse click the red cross.

{MORDORS_ENVOY_BODY}After the rumours of Khand’s betrayal found their way into your court, a heavy silence of uncertainty hanged over you and your circle of advisors. Or worst of all, stab you in the back… The question of what to do about this threat plagued your advisors for resources were also needed elsewhere, and they feared diverting resources into fighting Khand would lead to weaknesses on the front lines that would be exploited by your enemies.\n\nIt seemed there was no clear answer; but then the envoy from the Black Hand arrived, on a Morgul steed, he sprinted into the court of your palace, as if the Nazgûl themselves pursued him, and some feared that is what he was, one of the Nine. He strode into your throne room and threw off his coat, revealing he was not a Nazgûl as many feared but a mere mortal; a Black Númenórean of the Temple, clad in the fine garments of the Temple and extruding an aura of fear, one that demanded respect. And yet for all his dignity and grandeur, he panted, almost as if he had not stopped to rest on his journey to your capital.\n\nHe cried out “Lord of this land, I have been sent by the Chief Inquisitor Ancântar, the Black Hand of Sauron, who holds complete power in matters that concern the Blue Wizards. They have turned Khand against us and seek to undermine the victories we have gained with their allies; treacherous dwarves of the East. However, Ancântar requires assistance, hence, he requests that you aid him.”\n\nAfter agreeing to his request, for defeating Khand would aid in your security and safeguard your interests, he rushed out of the room and departed on his steed as quickly as he came; undoubtedly to return to his master.

Los muros que antaño ofrecían protección sirven ahora como muros de la eternidad a los defensores asesinados de las ornamentadas cavernas. {MORDOR_BLACKGATE_LOST_BODY}The Black Gate that stands watch over the withered land have been taken! Although slowed by the choking fumes and the gloom that hides the reassuring rays of Anor, they seem intent on marching further into the Lands of Shadow. No doubt they will need many more warriors if they hope to pass beyond the Isenmouthe, or come against the Dark Lord himself and siege the gates of the Dark Tower. This victory, though just a prelude, has bolstered the spirits of the Free People rejoicing that the Lord of Deception may now know fear. For Sauron it is a warning, that the Free People have power and valour still, and a will to resist him. He is not unassailable as he once supposed.
