¬##### Script: Faction Dunland - Regain Homeland
{DUNLAND_HOMELAND_START_BODY}En el 2510 T.E Gondor entregó nuestra patria, por ellos llamada Calenardhon, que más tarde se conoció como Rohan, a los sucios cabezas de paja que bajaron de Rhovanion. Nuestro pueblo fue expulsado y se asentó en Isengard. Desde entonces numerosas veces hemos intentado conquistar de nuevo nuestras tierras pero fracasamos... El primer hombre que heroicamente casi logra nuestro objetivo fue el hijo de Freca, Wulf, que tenía orígenes con nuestro pueblo. Uniendo a sus parientes de fuera de Rohan con enemigos de Gondor que habían desembarcado en las bocas de Lefnui e Isen, invadimos desde el Este, el Isen e Isengard, y finalmente Wulf tomó Edoras en 2758 T.E.. Con la ayuda del frío del largo invierno, el débil rey de Rohan, Helm Mano Martillo, murió durante nuestro asedio a Cuernavilla. Pero nosotros mismos nos vimos reducidos tras el Largo Invierno y finalmente Wulf fue rematado por las manos de Fréaláf. Tras la pérdida de Wulf fuimos expulsados de Isengard a las tierras salvajes del más allá y desde entonces los cabezas de paja han mantenido una fuerte fuerza en sus fronteras. Pero todavía hay muchos de los nuestros viviendo en Rohan, puros y mezclados, que han tenido que ocultar su deseo de reunirse con su gente que fue expulsada.\n\nPero los cabezas de paja y los señores de Gondor cometieron un error, ¡regalaron Isengard al Mago que ahora ha prometido suministrarnos las armas y armaduras que necesitamos y bajo el fuerte liderazgo de nuestro Caudillo Yagthak contraatacaremos y recuperaremos nuestras tierras! La guardia en la frontera de Rohan ya ha sido reducida debido al trabajo de los espías del Mago y su influencia en la corte de Edoras. Si logramos superar sus defensas y recuperar nuestras tierras nuestros parientes que aún viven allí podrán salir de sus escondites y unirse a nosotros con las tácticas de guerra aprendidas de la convivencia con los cabezas de paja.
{DUNLAND_HOMELAND_START_TITLE}¡Recupera nuestra antigua patria!
{DUNLAND_HOMELAND_BODY}Por fin, tras décadas de incursiones y escaramuzas, hemos conseguido lo que antes sólo soñábamos: ¡expulsar a los cabezas de paja y reasentarnos en nuestras legítimas tierras! Nuestros parientes, que han vivido aquí oprimidos durante cientos de años, se reúnen por fin con sus hermanos de sangre y pronto podremos desplegar nuevas unidades al estilo de los ejércitos opresores. Todo Dunland se regocija y nuestro cacique se ganará la gloria y el reconocimiento eternos a partir de hoy.
{DUNLAND_HOMELAND_TITLE}¡Patria recuperada!

¬ ######## Khazad-Dum
{KHAZAD_START_BODY}Durin, Eldest of The Seven Fathers (first awoken of the Children of Aulë), founded Khazad-dûm, the Dwarf Mansions, the heart of our ancient realm. Since the First Age we had thrived and prospered in these halls, for here we found mithril! Here also was our greatest friendship with the elves - in their kingdom of Hollin - and our own part in forging the Great Rings. In this wonder lay our undoing, as we dug too deep and awoke Durin's Bane, the spirit of shadow and flame - Valaraukar - a Balrog of Morgoth! A thousand years have passed since Durin's Bane drove us from our ancient home, and now our travel-worn expedition stands inside the Dimrill Gate, the doors of Moria, and the words of friendship are revealed under the moonlight once more. Be aware: evil lurks in the depths, and Durin’s Bane still haunts the deepest reaches of Moria.\n\nIt falls to you, lord, to return these great halls to their former glory. We are all stout veterans of many battles, but the possibility of reinforcements are slight and we have few or no friends on our borders. We must count on our martial prowess, hard earned against many a foe, and subtle diplomacy to secure our right to settle once more in these great halls of our forefathers. To the East, we face the Silvan Elves, who still blame our hunger for mithril upon the waking of the Balrog, fiercest of their ancient foes. They will not forgive us this easily. Despite our enmity in these dark times, it would be wise to try to ease the tension and gain their favour, but the decision remains yours, lord.\n\nTo the West and the North, there are the great Mines and Halls of Khazad-dûm and the wonders of the Mithril Forge where once our forefathers' hammers rang as our songs were sung and, from our deep breweries, there flowed rivers of our finest ales. By Sauron's will, those are now in the filth-ridden claws of the Goblins, our hated foe; cowards who will never have forgotten our axes and our battle wrath from the war of Vengeance and the slaughter of Azog upon the stairs of Azanulbizar. There will be no peace with those who slew our great King, Thrór, bearer of one of the last of the Seven Rings. Our axes cry out to drink the sweet nectar of hatred - to feast in the black poison of goblin blood. With war, pain and wrathful war-mask our axes' thirst for revenge shall never be sated - till the scourge of the Misty Mountains is complete, the last shield is smitten, the last severed goblin-head mounted upon an upthrust stake.\n\nIt would be wise to reconquer these regions, as many of the forges and storerooms remain in working condition - that will be of great help reforging the armour and weapons for our most mightiest warriors. Northward along the great Misty Mountains lie strongholds infested by wretched Goblins. One day - when we have regained our former strength – we shall move against these creatures and slay them, cleaning their festering corpses from under and atop those fine mountains!\n\nUntil that day lord, we have to endure many battles while working to restore the halls of Durin and our greatest Sires. Your loyal Dwarves stand ready; awaiting for your orders - ready to follow you on our quest to surpass our former glories, striding forth into the next chapter of our histories, striking out boldly against those who would arrest our great march through the pages of the histories of Middle-earth.\n\n“Baruk Khazâd! Khazâd ai-mênu!”
{KHAZAD_START_TITLE}Enanos de Khazad-dûm

{MORIA_RECLAIMED_SUCCESS_BODY}We have brought shame to all those who doubted us my Lord! Khazad-dûm is ours again! We shall begin repairing the halls, burying the dead and shoring up the defenses.\n\nWe should also look further afield and begin expanding our realm. The halls and chambers of this ancient place are vast and in need of much repair. The city itself will not provide enough resources and despite our successes, we are still stretched thin. We should send word to the other Dwarven clans of our success! Send diplomats to the Lonely Mountain, the Iron Hills, Thorin's Halls and if you deem it worth the journey, we have heard rumour that many of our folk from the Red Mountains now reside in a settlement called 'Qufah', far, far to the south-east.\n\nAs long as Khazad-dûm is in our hands and our kinsmen know it, many dwarves will slowly begin to migrate here and help us rebuild our once great kingdom. All will know of the great victory of the Dwarves of Khazad-dûm!
{MORIA_RECLAIMED_SUCCESS_TITLE}A New Era

{MORIA_CROWNING_BODY}There has been many years of relative peace within the walls of Khazad-dûm. Our renewed kingdom is growing and the goblin attacks are waning. Despite this, our people grow restless that our kingdom has no King.\n\nTherefore, our highlord is to be crowned by the stars as did our great father, Durin I, next year. He insists on going to the Mirrormere and Durin's Stone alone.\n\nSend our highlord to the Mirrormere, just east of Khazad-Dum and await his coronation.
{MORIA_CROWNING_TITLE}Coronando a nuestro rey

{MORIA_CORONATION_BODY}According to the tales of old, after Durin the Deathless had awoken at Mount Gundabad in the north of the Misty Mountains, he travelled south until he came upon this lake. He looked down in it and saw a crown above his head, being the reflection of stars above. Taking the crown as a sign, he founded the great city of Khazad-dûm beneath the mountains that fed Mirrormere. A pillar called Durin's Stone was placed along the banks of the lake to mark the event and it is here where we crown our king, our highlord!
{MORIA_CORONATION_TITLE}The King of Khazad-dûm

{VAULT_OF_URUK_BODY}The Treasure Vault of Uruktharbûn was a wonder of the ancient Dwarven world. The now forgotten Dwarves of yore laid mighty spells upon this chamber and filled it with a profound darkness. No light may shine in the vault, not torchlight nor candlelight nor crystal lamp — save perhaps some greater magic, like the light of a Silmaril of Fëanor. In Durin's day, the vault-keepers were a hereditary caste, trained from a young age to navigate the lightless halls and return with the treasures sought. Some accounts say they grew their beards especially long, and used them like a spool of thread to find their way back to the doors.\n\nNow, the vault is a maze of corridors, and many of the chambers contain cunning death-traps, made both by the goblins and naturally by time. A great many treasures likely still remain here and if we are lucky, the Goblin scum have not managed to soil the mighty walls of the Grand Vault which lies at the end of countless tunnels and chambers. If we ever hope to uncover it's secrets, we should get to work immediately.\n\nTo begin exploring the vault, select Khazad-Dum West and open the new tab in the Half Scroll.
{VAULT_OF_URUK_TITLE}The Vault of Uruktharbûn

{VAULT_OF_URUK_OPEN_BODY}At long last, thanks to our hard work and diligent planning, we have cleared and mapped again the many chambers and corridors of the Vault of Uruktharbûn. The only thing that remained was to uncover the secrets of the Grand Vault, for Khazad-dûm was arguably the greatest kingdom of Dwarves ever known in Middle-earth and many wondered what the proudest Dwarven kings would keep hidden away in it's mightiest vault. What exactly we found, however, was most unexepected. For the Grand Vault did not have gilded halls or mountains of gems. It did not sparkle and shimmer. It was empty, cold, dark. Only a single shaft of light from some far, unknown source bore down upon a small pedastal in the centre of the room. Upon this pedastal, a book - "A History of Dwarves".\n\nThe feeling amongst those who had slaved away for many a month in these dark and dangerous halls was palpable. Dissapointment and even anger, was rife. For what use was a book? A book cannot stay the blade of an orc. It cannot be tempered or wrought. And you too shared these thoughts. That was, until you read it. It occured to you that this book was placed here and diligently protected, for a reason. Whoever came before you here in these hallowed halls deemed this simple book, the most valuable treasure of all Khazad-dûm. This was a book, made for kings. Made by kings. By Náin I, Durin VI, many kings of yore known only amongst the most scholarly and even by Durin the Deathless himself. You realize there is wisdom here, wisdom more valuable than even the most resplendant and lustrous mithril. The book is a reminder of everything your people have endured. The triumphs, the trials and everything in between. The successes and the failures. Stories of love and hatred. Reminders of who you are and why you stand here now, in these hallowed halls. For you are not the first Lord of Moria and you doubt you will be the last. Thanks to this book and the wisdom of your forefathers, you can guarantee their legacy will live on through you and your sons. Long live Khazad-Dûm, long live the Dwarves!
{VAULT_OF_URUK_OPEN_TITLE}The Grand Vault of Uruktharbûn

¬##### Script: Forsaken Inn
{FORSAKEN_INN_INTRO_BODY}On the eastern edge of the Bree-land there is a ramshackle wooden hovel that is easily mistaken by the casual observer for a forgotten and abandoned cabin, rotting by the road. It is only the steady stream of unsavoury characters trickling in and out of the crooked front door that reveals it to be an inn – if only in the most broad sense of the word.\n\nAptly called the Forsaken Inn, this establishment attracts patrons fitting its appearance and name. Criminals, desperate wanderers, and those who are best avoided seek refuge here, drinking the worst kind of swill, and sleeping on flea infested mattresses set out beneath the leaky roof of the common room. Those who complain of a draft in the evening are given a lice-ridden blanket and charged an extra silver penny for the privilege.\n\nEven the ill-favoured Rangers of the North do not often go here. The proprietor, who refuses to give his name even to paying customers and is then called simply ‘Jack the Forsaken’ by most, has no concerns about who enters his establishment, as long as they have silver to spend. Unfortunately, those with coins to pay had best sit with their backs to the wall, lest a patron make quick use of a dagger and relieve them of their wealth.\n\nUntil you "renovate" the Forsaken Inn, it will continue to be a hub of activity for unsavoury men and their accomplices. Small rebel armies will gather here, and the inn will be a place of refuge for those who would do harm to the Free Peoples of Eriador.
{FORSAKEN_INN_INTRO_TITLE}The Forsaken Inn

{FORSAKEN_INN_REPAIRED_BODY}After gathering the gold and finding a few journeymen brave enough to venture so far from Bree, the Council and the men of Bree have managed to bring order to the rogues and footpads that plagued these lands. With the final tile laid down on the roof, the rennovations to the inn have been completed, clean beds and all.\nTraffic along this road to The Angle will now surely increase along with the size of our coin purse, much to Jack's dismay. We needn't be wary of any more rebels and if we're lucky, we might even make a few friends from what's left of the patrons. The future of the Inn is now in good hands.
{FORSAKEN_INN_REPAIRED_TITLE}A New Start

{FORSAKEN_INN_UPGRADED_BODY}Over the years, ever since the Forsaken Inn was brought under the yoke of the Council, it has grown and flourished more than any could imagine.\nSince the passing of it's previous proprietor Jack, the inn has opened it's doors to a wide variety of guests. No longer a hovel with maggot ridden mattresses and stale ale, it now stands as a bastion against any who would threaten the Bree-land and it's people. Mercenaries, ruffians, hedge knights and merchants line it's walls, eager to tell a tale of their latest adventure or sale.\nNow a meeting place for any able bodied man, it can support the upkeep of a small company of Bree-land natives where they both can relax and spend their hard earned coin but also be a reminder to the patrons who is still in charge...
{FORSAKEN_INN_UPGRADED_TITLE}The Forsaken Keep

¬##### Events when factions are revived
{SICILY_REVIVED_BODY}By machinations of its allies of old or the rebellious acclamation of a new Steward, the Kingdom of Gondor has returned. The banner of the White Tree flies once more in wind atop the tower, and it's guards stand ready to defend with their lives the renewed beauty of the city of the men of Númenor.\n\nTo the ringing of silver trumpets, the restored Steward makes a joyful proclamation: "Never again, shall the lands of my people fall into enemy hands!"
{SICILY_REVIVED_TITLE} The White City Restored!

{DENMARK_REVIVED_BODY}By machinations of its allies of old or strength in arms of a rebellion against the Shadow, the Principality of Dol Amroth has returned. Swans fly once more atop the reclaimed Titith Aer, its walls stand well-manned and Seaward companies ride the Belfalas sweeping their heartland free of evil.\n\nMessangers ride to those bastions still remaining: The Strength of the Faithful is not yet spent! And whereever men march still to war against the Shadow, there the Swan shall fly in the van!
{DENMARK_REVIVED_TITLE}Dol Amroth Renewed!

{TURKS_REVIVED_BODY}By machinations of their allies of old or the stepping forth of a new Chieftain in rebellion against the Shadow, the Dúnedain of Arnor have returned. Though no banners fly to make their ascendency, nor trumpets herald their coming, lost sons of forgotten kings once more go a-wandering, protecting from evil things folk who are heedless. \n\nWhilst the Line of Isildur may be extinguished the memory of their last stand gives hope to the Dunedain, though they keep none for themselves. To friend and foe alike the Rangers sing now bittersweet: "Remember the Blood of Númenor! Tûl Acharn! Tûl Acharn!"
{TURKS_REVIVED_TITLE}The Oath of Elendil Remembered!

{RUSSIA_REVIVED_BODY}By machinations of their allies of old or the seizing of Ar-Pharazôn's mantle by a warlord of the kingsmen, the Ar-Adûnaim have returned. The Haven lands of Umbar know blackplated tread once more. Dark sails stalk the sea lanes and the sound of the waves is as a warcry to they who stand against the reclamation.\n\nTake heart or fear at this news, the true sons of Númenor care not. The Ar-Adûnaim have returned and what could not be claimed by birthright, shall by the spear be won!
{RUSSIA_REVIVED_TITLE}The Ar-Adûnaim Return!

{NORMANS_REVIVED_BODY}By the efforts of its allies of old or the unlikely valor in the fray of a new captain of Hobbits and Men, the Bree-land has been restored to the Free peoples. Travellers are once again welcomed into the hospitality of the prancing pony and the restored Council of Bree makes preperations to turn ploughshares into swords, farmhands into soldiers, that all those who love peace might live in it once more.\n\nThe Hill of Bree stands, with increasing confidence, with all who tend the light afore the dark. Let it not be said that Middle-Earth be lost, while but one of the Free Folk lives within it!
{NORMANS_REVIVED_TITLE}The Bree-land Reclaimed!

{AZTECS_REVIVED_BODY}Whether by the machinations of the White Wizard or the independent claiming, in Blood, of the Wooden Crown of the Gurach, the Dunlendings hold their land anew. Sacred Crebain flock, in the sky and on silk, and the clanhost musters. The take down heirloom weapons from their walls, that they might awaken smouldering feuds to new fire.\n\nThe bitterness of long centuries shall be slaked in Forgoil blood. Let war drums herald the wailing of thine women! The Dunlendings are come!
{AZTECS_REVIVED_TITLE}Dunland Resurgent!

{TEUTONIC_ORDER_REVIVED_BODY}By the machinations of a foreign power or simply a new warlord taking the Moot hall for his hold, men now speak of a new force in the lands of Enedwaith. Though their Chieftain cannot yet claim mastership of any but the Lugiu, perhaps here is one who can unit the fractious clans of the greyflood, against those who would see them divided.\n\nLet all who would stake a claim in Eriador be warned thus: The Gwathló flows anew and it's sons shall not be denied!
{TEUTONIC_ORDER_REVIVED_TITLE}Enedwaith Anew!

{SPAIN_REVIVED_BODY}By the machinations of the Lord of the Rings, or by the riding out of the desert of a warlord to equal the Kings of old, the Haradrim once more have a worthy leader. The Black Serpent banner looms again over Faen'obel, and lords from all across the heartland of Haruzan journey to the old capital to pledge their blood to the warleader.\n\nThe Desert kills the weak, but keeps the land for it's people. Hail the Serpent Ascendant, a new dawn, and the Great Eye!
{SPAIN_REVIVED_TITLE}The Serpent Banner raised once more!

{KHAND_REVIVED_BODY}By machinations of the Dark Lord, the Blue Wizards, or simply the ambitions of a new warlord, the Khanship of the Variags is claimed once more. Tribesmen journey to Sturlutsa seeking admission to the Kheshig, Windriders carry the news of the Khan's ascendency to aged Horse Lords in their Gers. There is a new order in the lands of the Variags, and the Khan's word is law.\n\nTrepidation shall be felt in the hearts of many who fight, both for and against the Shadow. Hoofbeats thunder in the east once more and though their foe may be uncertain, their victory is not! Whether the Sons of the Steppe water their horses on the banks of the Anduin or the shores of the Núrnen, they ride!
{KHAND_REVIVED_TITLE}A New Khanate!

{VENICE_REVIVED_BODY}By the machinations of Barad-dûr or the indpendent raising of the Dragon-Banner by a would-be Lôke-Khan, the fortunes of the men of Rhûn are restored. Mistrand, freshly reconsecrated by Priests of the Dragon-Cult, is once again to be the seat of mastery of the ruler of all the Easterlings.\n\nWith the Spear and Sword, the Bow and the eternal Flame, the men of Rhûn pledge themselves once more to the designs of the Great Eye. Let the Men of the Lakes, the Dwarves of the Mountains, and the wretched Elfkind of the Woods take heed! Waters boil, stones melt, and all forests burn before the Dragon!
{VENICE_REVIVED_TITLE}The Dragonfires Rekindled!

{NORWAY_REVIVED_BODY}Whether by the sponsoring of the King under the Mountain, or others amongst the Free peoples, the Dwarves have once again returned to Khazad-Dûm. The Ill-fated dead of Balin's expedition, those that could be recovered, have been laid to rest in tombs that would honour Durin himself. The forges are relit, the deeps delved, and the Legion reformed.\n\nThere is little joy in these new inhabitants of Moria, however. The Dwarves of Khazad-Dûm take up axes with grim resolve, counting afresh new scores to be settled, standing firm against all the Shadow in arms. In Victory or Defeat, theirs is to be the last reclamation!
{NORWAY_REVIVED_TITLE}Khazad-Dûm Resettled

{MOORS_REVIVED_BODY}Whether by the efforts of his Kinsmen, or those of the White Council, A King once again reigns under the Mountain. For the second time in its sad history are the halls of Erebor reclaimed, not now from a ravening dragon, but from more mundane foes of the Dwarves. Volunteers flock to the Kings banner, and the Men of Dale send word they will stand with their friends of old once more.\n\nLet all true sons of Durin take heart! The High King returns, and there shall be a reckoning! Khazâd! Khazâd! Khazâd ai mênu!
{MOORS_REVIVED_TITLE}A King Under the Mountain!

{HUNGARY_REVIVED_BODY}At the behest of Kinsmen or Friends, the wandering of the Dwarves of the Blue mountains is at an end. Thorin's hall once again knows the footsteps of busy Dwarves, the high places are made safe, the dead mourned, and a cold fury settled within the hearts of the Firebeards and Broadbeams.\n\nCome all who would try to take the Ered Luin from their rightful owners. The Axes of the Dwarves shall slake their thirst in unclean blood!
{HUNGARY_REVIVED_TITLE}The Ered Luin retaken!

{SCOTLAND_REVIVED_BODY}By intervention from under the lonely Mountain, or from help across the Peaks of Mist, from under the Boughs of Mirkwood or by the sole efforts of a hero of the Lake-Folk, the Kingdom of Dale has been restored. A man from amongst the Bardings once more treats with Dwarf and Elf as equal, old alliances are affirmed anew, and all know that the strength of the Northmen lingers.\n\nTake heart, enemies of the Dark! The Sons of Rhovanion fight ever on!
{SCOTLAND_REVIVED_TITLE}The Kingdom of Dale Restored!

{MILAN_REVIVED_BODY}Edoras, Brego's Hall, once more bears the King's Banner. An heir of Helm is raised up and in the plains of Rohan, the Éored gather. The Old Oaths are remembered and awaken in the Rohirrim their old lust for war, fanned to life by the casting off of defeat and shame, for good and all.\n\n'Where now the Horse and the Rider?' ask some still, ere the Riddermark fell. 'Here are we' cries the King 'and lo, the sun rises. Now for wrath, now for ruin, and the Red Dawn upon our foes. Forth Eorlingas!'
{MILAN_REVIVED_TITLE}Here Now the Horse and the Rider!

{BYZANTIUM_REVIVED_BODY}By the hand of Elves or Men, the Vine-Regents have been restored to the rule of Dorwinion. Chosen once more from amongst the finest of the Rouadengi nobility, and owed the fealty of the Kugath, the Léofthiuda and the Avari of the forests, here is a man to reclaim the Sea of Rhun for the Free Peoples of Dorwinion.\n\nMay Wealth, Wine and Victory flow ever in the realm of the Vine-Lords! The Vintner-Court is restored and it shall have its due!
{BYZANTIUM_REVIVED_TITLE}The Vine-Regency restored!

{TIMURIDS_REVIVED_BODY}Whether under the auspices of others or rebellion in the fierce spirit of Beorn, the Vale of Anduin is restored to the Free folk. From their holdfast in the hall of their forefather, the Beornings give shelter to Woodsmen, Stoors and the horsemen of the Éothéod. The new Huntsmaster intends to make all the vale safe once more.\n\nLo now do the Bearmen wake! Hear them roar, in high pass and valley deep! Theirs is the Fury, undimmed, unbroken!
{TIMURIDS_REVIVED_TITLE}The Vale Restored!

{MONGOLS_REVIVED_BODY}Whether by foreign help or the exploits in war of a last captain of the Nandor, the Elvenking's halls are inhabited once more. Elves make their home in Greenwood again, and attempt to drive back the mirk that encroaches upon its eaves. Refugees and settlers have been invited from amongst all Elves whom would live in freedom, and many amognst the Avari of Dorwinion have answered the call. Though the line of Oropher may be extinguished, the memory of the Sindar is not forgotten, and many Sindar from Lindon have journeyed east over the Misty Mountains.\n\nThough the shadow of Dol Guldur may rest still upon the mirkwood, in the north at least there is again a power to challenge it. The Woodland Realm is a-marching and the song of elves is heard beneath the leaves once more!
{MONGOLS_REVIVED_TITLE}The Woodland Realm Renewed!

{IRELAND_REVIVED_BODY}By the efforts of those whom remain on the White Council, or by a last remnant of the marchwardens, the Mallorn forests of Lothlorien are host to elves once more! In great sorrow is this endeavour taken, for the loss of the Lord Celeborn and the Lady Galadriel is fit to break the strongest amongst the Eldar, and many of their kindred had not the heart to bear it. These are the latter days of Lothlorien, and all left know it.\n\nThe Elves of the wood enjoin no more in song. On their faces do they wear Ash, in their chests do they breath wroth, and carry bloody vengeance in their hands. Let all who remain of the Galadhrim reap a bloody eulogy amongst the servants of Sauron! The Lady is dead, and we go on now without hope, unto the breaking of the World!
{IRELAND_REVIVED_TITLE}Lothlorien's Rage Awakened!

{DORWINION_ENCIRCLE_SEA_AI_TITLE}Sea of Rhûn Encircled

Never in living memory, nor distant history, has such a feat been accomplished by civilized man. Even the ancient Kingdom of Numenor could only boast a gentle touch upon this land. Now the Sea and all its fertile coastlands rest tightly within the fist of the Vintiner-Court. The world watches with bated breath; how will this young nation grow and expand with its new found power? Will the Regent remain satisfied with these new holding and seek the path of peace? Or is this the birth of a new Empire on middle-earth?\n\nWhatever the future holds, it is clear; Dorwinion is rising…

{DORWINION_DRAGON_CULT_INTEGRATE_AI_TITLE}The Dragon is Born Anew
{DORWINION_DRAGON_CULT_INTEGRATE_AI_BODY}For many centuries the Dragon Cult, or Cult of Urulóke, has dominated the lands of Rhûn and made itself integral within its ruling classes and military establishment. Now that Rhûn has been defeated, and its lands fallen into the hands of Dorwinion, the Regent has made a an important decision regarding the future of Rhûn and Dorwinion…\n\nHe has decided to accept the Dragon Cult!\n\nIn essence this will allow the Court to more effectively control the newly conquered territories, as the cult has a great reach of influence that is already established. It is a practical choice, but some worry that the Dragon Cult is too powerful and will be bring corruption into Dorwinion.\n\nThere are many within the Vintner-Court who are already moving to resist this dynamic shift, the Regent has drawn his lot and must bare the consequences…
{DORWINION_DRAGON_CULT_OUTLAW_AI_TITLE}The Dragon is Extinguished
{DORWINION_DRAGON_CULT_OUTLAW_AI_BODY}For many centuries the Dragon Cult, or Cult of Urulóke, has dominated the lands of Rhûn and made itself an integral part of its ruling classes and military establishment. Now that Rhûn has been defeated, and its lands fallen into the hands of Dorwinion, the Regent has made a an important decision regarding the future of Rhûn and Dorwinion…\n\nHe has decided to extinguish the Dragon Cult!\n\nIn essence this will prolong the conflict in Rhûn, as the cult has a great reach and influence throughout the land. Each cultist is a powerful warrior, and to face down an army of these men will require great sacrifice for the forces of Dorwinion. A final battle is drawn, and the future of the East hangs in the balance.\n\nHowever difficult it will be to erase the Dragon Cult is overshadowed by the unanimous cheers of joy that echoed through the Vintner-Court this day. Dorwinion itself is more united than ever!
{DORWINION_REFORMS_AI_TITLE}The Regentsmen Reforms
{DORWINION_REFORMS_AI_BODY}Dorwinion is changing. Their days of wallowing in the summer sun by the poppies and juniper berries is like a fleeting dream. Now only war occupies their mind and oh how heavy it weighs.\n\nAs was proposed many a year ago, by Order of the Vintner Court and the High Council of Helcarin, the Regentsmen Reforms have finally been deemed complete. Dorwinion's armies have never been made up of professional, full-time soldiers, but were ever an amalgam of the forces of the many distinct people making up the realm itself. Now, with such power and wealth at their beck and call, the position of Regent has expanded substantially in authority.\n\nBy Regents decree, the court's coffers pay for the equipment and training of men, drawn from all the cities and villages that sit on the coast of the sea, men who are under arms all year round, and ready to fight whenever called upon. Advisors from other realms have been paid to help organize this reform. This new force is split into four branches, of infantry, archers, cavalry and naval. This step marks Dorwinion's evolution into a polity truly able to effectively project its power outward, and both friend and foe should be wary of shrewd merchants with such a force at their command.
